By leveraging the frozen VidMark decoder, this process enables VGMs to synthesize videos carrying an imperceptible, robust, and model-specific watermark. Finally, two verification frameworks are established to perform synthetic video verification and model ownership verification. Extensive experiments on representative VGMs demonstrate that the proposed scheme achieves over 99% watermark extraction accuracy and 100% verification accuracy on both tasks, with negligible impact on video generation quality. Furthermore, the watermarks exhibit strong robustness against a comprehensive range of video-level and model-level attacks.

</details>


### [101] [Collapse, Not Complexity: Failure-Conditioned Decomposition Repair for End-to-End Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23592)
*Xingyu Lin,Dehui Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发现：在文档解析中，“启动推理模式”并非依据“页面复杂度”来决策的好策略。推理模式常让质量下降且成本升高；真正该做的是从普通解析的失败迹象中检测“崩溃”，对页面做投影分解并分区重解析，能以更低额外token成本带来显著收益，并在多检查点与大规模基准上复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界逐步在端到端文档解析器中加入可选“推理模式”以处理疑难页面，但其启用条件往往以“页面复杂度”为依据。作者质疑这一前提：推理是否真的对复杂页面更有效？能否被输入特征准确预测？在计算成本与质量权衡下，需要系统性证据指导“何时用推理”。

Method: 1) 构建180页的熵分层发现样本；固定一个4B模型检查点。2) 比较普通模式与推理模式：质量差异与token开销；并用仅基于输入的预测器（先验注册）预测推理收益方向，在留出集评估。3) 分析“崩溃”现象（普通解析输出退化为高重复/无效文本），度量其与布局熵/输出token的关系，以及推理模式对其修复率。4) 提出修复方法：从普通解析的trace检测崩溃，按投影将页面分块，再对各区域独立重解析。5) 在3个检查点与额外1,175页基准上进行冻结参数复现实验，报告质量提升与token倍率。

Result: - 推理模式总体使质量均值下降2.21分，同时token增至1.54倍。- 仅基于输入的模型无法预测推理的“正收益”，留出集AUROC=0.47（与随机无异）。- 推理的少数收益集中在普通解析已“崩溃”的页面；这些页面反而布局熵更低，却因退化性重复耗费19倍token；加倍预算也无效。- 切换到推理模式很少修复崩溃：83%在推理下仍复现。- 提出的“检测崩溃-投影分解-分区重解析”方法：在发现集上带来+1.40 Overall（95% CI [0.68, 2.16]），token仅增至1.13倍；在三检查点复现；在剩余1,175页基准上（全参冻结）提升+2.41（CI [1.64, 3.46]）。

Conclusion: “页面复杂度”不是启用推理模式的有效信号；推理往往降质增耗，且无法稳定修复解析崩溃。相反，应从普通解析trace中检测崩溃，再进行基于投影的页面分解与分区重解析，以小幅token增量获得显著且可复现的质量提升。

Abstract: End-to-end document parsers increasingly offer an optional reasoning mode for complex pages. On a 180-page entropy-stratified discovery sample with one frozen 4B checkpoint, complexity is the wrong decision variable. Reasoning lowers mean quality by 2.21 Overall at 1.54x tokens; a preregistered input-only model cannot predict its signed benefit (held-out AUROC 0.47, indistinguishable from chance). The benefit concentrates on pages whose ordinary pass has already collapsed, and they do not look complex: shared collapses have lower layout entropy than healthy ones yet consume 19x the tokens as degenerate repetition that doubling the budget does not cure. Switching modes rarely repairs them: 83% recur under reasoning. We instead detect collapse from the ordinary-pass trace, decompose the page by projection, and re-parse each region. Repair gains 1.40 Overall (95% CI [0.68, 2.16]) at 1.13x tokens, replicates across three checkpoints, and, with all parameters frozen, gains 2.41 (CI [1.64, 3.46]) on the remaining 1,175 benchmark pages.

</details>


### [102] [StyleAT: Defending Face Recognition Against Semantic Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23596)
*Ben Shapira,Roi Cohen,Shang-Tse Chen,Mahmood Sharif*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出BoundStyle，一种在StyleGAN潜空间进行的高效语义对抗攻击，并基于此构建高效对抗训练方法StyleAT，在多数据集与多模型上显著提升鲁棒性且速度较现SOTA快约9.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人脸识别模型对“语义编辑”式的对抗扰动（如轻微变老、姿态变化）极为脆弱；但现有强力攻击计算开销大，不适合用于对抗训练与实际防御开发，亟需一种既强又快、可用于训练的语义攻击方法。

Method: 1) 设计BoundStyle：在StyleGAN丰富潜空间中施加受约束的语义偏移，以最大化人脸识别误分类率，同时控制编辑幅度与计算预算；其优化过程较以往方法更高效。2) 基于BoundStyle提出StyleAT：利用低预算的BoundStyle变体作为训练时的对手样本生成器，实现高效对抗训练，并可泛化抵御更强和未见过的语义攻击。

Result: 在两个训练外数据集与七个模型上评测：BoundStyle攻击成功率高且较SOTA攻击约快9.5倍；StyleAT在多种设置下相对常见防御显著提升鲁棒准确率，并对最先进语义攻击表现更强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过在StyleGAN潜空间进行高效语义对抗编辑，既能快速构造强攻击（BoundStyle），又能据此进行高效对抗训练（StyleAT），实现对未见语义攻击的更好防御，兼顾攻击强度与训练可用性。

Abstract: With face-recognition models now embedded in everyday authentication and surveillance, recent works have pinpointed a critical weakness: these models remain acutely vulnerable to adversarial semantic edits. I.e., adversarially produced semantic alterations to the input, such as slight aging or pose changes, can induce misclassifications. Certain existing attacks are powerful, but they can be computationally costly, rendering them inadequate for developing defenses (e.g., through adversarial training). To fill the gap, we introduce BoundStyle, a potent semantic attack operating in StyleGAN's rich latent space to maximize misclassification rates. Notably, BoundStyle achieves high attack success rates while being ${\sim}{\times}9.5$ faster than existing state-of-the-art attacks, making it suitable for adversarial training. Building on BoundStyle, we develop StyleAT, an efficient adversarial training scheme that incorporates low-budget attack variants yet defends against stronger and unseen semantic attacks. We evaluate on two datasets unseen during training and seven models, and find that StyleAT boosts robust accuracy against state-of-the-art attacks and outperforms common defenses in various settings.

</details>


### [103] [PETR: Prompt Ensembling with Training-free Routing for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23600)
*Weihan Cai,Hao Tan,Xinping Gao,Shibiao Xu,Jun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PETR：一种带训练免路由的提示集成方法，在CLIP上用双提示架构同时兼顾已见类判别与未见类泛化，并在推理时依据统计相似度自适应选择提示分支，达成SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示学习能高效适配VLM，但提升已见类精度常以牺牲未见类泛化为代价；MoE式路由又存在训练不稳与负载不均等问题，缺乏可解释的自适应选择机制。

Method: 设计双提示架构：两个互补提示分别从不同数据/目标学习，一个偏重已见类判别，另一个偏重未见类泛化；共享冻结的CLIP骨干。在训练时同时微调两条提示并从训练集logits收集统计量；推理时计算测试样本与已见数据的统计相似度，采用训练免的自适应路由将样本分配到更合适的提示分支，从而实现提示集成。

Result: 在11个基准数据集上，对已见与未见类均优于既有方法，整体达到新的SOTA表现。

Conclusion: 基于统计相似度的训练免路由结合双提示，兼顾已见判别和未见泛化，提供可解释且稳健的选择机制，避免MoE常见病灶，并在多基准上验证其有效性。

Abstract: Prompt learning efficiently adapts vision-language models (VLMs) to downstream tasks, but gains on seen classes often come at the expense of generalization to unseen classes. To address this limitation, we propose prompt ensembling with training-free routing (PETR), whose key innovation is a carefully designed dual-prompt architecture: two complementary prompts are learned from different data and objectives to emphasize seen class discrimination and unseen-class generalization, respectively. During training, both prompts are fine-tuned using a shared frozen CLIP backbone, and statistical information is collected from the training set logits. At inference time, we determine the similarity of each test sample to seen data, and route the sample to the most appropriate prompt branch. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first prompt tuning framework that performs training-free adaptive routing based on statistical similarity. This design provides an interpretable routing signal and avoids common MoE-style routing pathologies, such as router training instability and load imbalance. Extensive experiments on 11 benchmark datasets demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms previous methods on both seen and unseen classes, achieving new state-of-the-art results.

</details>


### [104] [PREM: Prefix-Steered Recurrent Memory for Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23601)
*Siru Zhong,Qiongyan Wang,Xiaohui Lv,Yuzheng Zhuang,Shuai Tao,Wulong Liu,Haohuan Fu,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PREM：一种无需记忆token的长视频理解框架，写入阶段将视频蒸馏到小型关联状态，读取阶段用问题条件化的K/V调制引导前缀，使冻结VLM在严格视觉预算下更准确、更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解需要在严格token预算下捕捉短暂且关键的视觉证据。现有方法依赖帧压缩、追加记忆token或改KV缓存，带来额外开销、推理延迟或破坏冻结模型的稳定性，且难以实现一次写入、多次查询。

Method: 引入Prefix-Steered Recurrent Memory（PREM）。将视频摄取与问答解耦：1）Recurrent writer将连续视频流蒸馏为约256 KiB、含多槽的关联状态（memory），无需在提示中追加记忆token；2）在预填充阶段，依据问题对该状态进行读取，将记忆派生的K/V调制量叠加到原有的非视觉前缀，从而“引导前缀”，实现对冻结VLM的K/V转向；3）支持一次写入、多次查询，无需在解码阶段循环读写。仅微调约0.24%主干参数，峰值显存增加约0.03 GiB。

Result: 在6个长视频基准（离线与流式末端场景）中，于所有视觉预算下均优于冻结基线。在16帧受限预算上，基于Qwen2.5-VL-3B宏平均精度+3.06%，其中动作反义识别+11.0%，局部needle检索+9.9%。

Conclusion: PREM在不增加提示token与不改动解码循环的前提下，通过前缀引导的K/V调制实现记忆高效复用，显著提升严格预算下的长视频理解性能与可扩展性，并以极小的参数/显存开销实现高性价比部署。

Abstract: Long-video understanding must capture transient visual evidence under strict token budgets, yet existing methods compress frames, append memory tokens, or alter internal key-value (KV) caches. We introduce Prefix-Steered Recurrent Memory (PREM), a memory-token-free framework for frozen vision-language models (VLMs). PREM separates video ingestion from query answering: a recurrent writer distills visual streams into a compact 256 KiB multi-slot associative state, while a question-conditioned readout adds memory-derived key/value (K/V) steering modulations to existing non-visual prompt prefixes during prefill. This enables write-once, query-many inference without extra prompt tokens or decoding recurrence. Across six long-video benchmarks in offline and streaming end-of-stream settings, PREM consistently outperforms frozen baselines at every evaluated visual budget. Under a constrained budget of 16 frames, PREM improves macro-average accuracy by 3.06% on Qwen2.5-VL-3B, with gains of 11.0% on action antonym identification and 9.9% on localized needle retrieval. These gains require tuning 0.24% of backbone parameters at 0.03 GiB of peak GPU memory overhead.

</details>


### [105] [Beyond UV Mapping: Mesh Texture Compression via Surface-Aligned Texture Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23606)
*Jianqiang Wang,Junhui Hou,Siyu Ren,Weiyao Lin,Wenping Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TexF与3DNTC：用表面对齐的稀疏体素纹理场替代UV展开，以更高压缩效率与实时渲染支持，较UV方法在MPEG与AOM基准上取得更优率失真表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网格纹理压缩依赖2D UV图集，存在分片不连续与映射开销，破坏3D相关性并限制编码效率；需要一种既能高分辨率表达、又便于压缩与直接表面查询的新表示。

Method: 1) TexF：从网格表面派生稀疏体素，按表面对齐组织纹理属性，保留局部3D相关性；体素位置可由解码网格重建，无需单独传输；复用成熟3D属性编解码器进行比特流压缩。2) 3DNTC：面向GPU的压缩方案，使用量化哈希特征+轻量解码器，在表面位置随机访问重建；支持可微渲染以在图像空间联合细化体素属性与神经压缩场。

Result: 在MPEG与AOM网格压缩基准上，相比代表性的UV方案取得更优平均率失真（RD）性能；3DNTC还能实现实时渲染。

Conclusion: 表面对齐的稀疏体素纹理场与GPU友好的神经压缩相结合，可提升纹理压缩效率与渲染可用性，减少UV相关开销，并在标准基准上验证了优越性。

Abstract: Mesh texture compression typically relies on 2D UV atlases, whose chart discontinuities and mapping overhead can limit coding efficiency. To tackle this challenge, we introduce TexF, a surface-aligned texture field that organizes texture attributes in sparse voxels derived from the mesh surface. This representation supports high-resolution textures while preserving local 3D correlations for compression and enabling direct surface queries. For bitstream compression, TexF reuses established 3D attribute codecs, with voxel locations reconstructed from the decoded mesh without separate transmission. For GPU-resident compression, we develop 3DNTC, which combines quantized hash features with a lightweight decoder for random-access reconstruction at surface positions. Differentiable rendering enables image-space refinement of both voxel attributes and compressed neural fields. Experiments on the MPEG and AOM mesh compression benchmarks demonstrate improved average rate-distortion performance over representative UV-based methods for both bitstream and GPU-resident compression. 3DNTC also supports real-time rendering.

</details>


### [106] [Compact Low-Cost Hyperspectral Imaging via Angular-to-Spectral Diversity Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23619)
*Kazuma Fujiwara,Takuya Funatomi,Kazuya Kitano,Yuki Fujimura,Yasuhiro Mukaigawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于角度到光谱多样性转换的单拍高光谱成像系统，用渐缩万花筒产生多视角、副视场配合贴附的双折射滤光片将视角差异映射为不同光谱透过函数，获得互补观测，从而在不依赖学习的前提下稳定、快速重建；系统低成本、紧凑、无需中继光学，组件均为现成器件，并通过条件数准则优化滤光片配置，在仿真与实测中验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有快照式高光谱方案往往在稳定重建、成本与体积之间难以兼顾：有的依赖复杂光机或深度学习重建（计算重负、鲁棒性差），有的需要级联/扫描模块（体积大、成本高）。作者希望在保持快照成像的同时，提高反演病态性的条件，获得更稳定、快速、低成本且紧凑的系统。

Method: 利用“角度→光谱”多样性转换：1）渐缩万花筒在同一探测面上生成多个具有不同入射角的复制视图；2）将双折射滤光片直接贴附于探测端，使其对不同入射方向产生不同、互补的光谱透过曲线（视角相关通道响应）；3）形成简单的逐像素线性观测模型，基于非学习的线性反演快速重建；4）以观测矩阵的条件数为指标，选择与优化双折射滤光片配置；5）在仿真与真实数据上进行验证。

Result: 在不使用中继光学或级联模块、仅用现成器件的前提下，实现单次曝光的高光谱采集；由多视角通道带来的互补谱响应显著改善了逆问题条件数，较传统配置获得更稳定、噪声鲁棒的重建；线性、像素级模型使重建速度快、无需训练；实验（合成与实测）验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 角度到光谱的多样性转换结合渐缩万花筒与双折射滤光片，可在紧凑、低成本光机下实现稳定、单拍高光谱重建；基于条件数的器件配置策略可指导实际系统设计，展示了无需学习即可获得高质量重建的潜力。

Abstract: Snapshot hyperspectral imaging avoids sequential scanning, but systems that jointly achieve stable reconstruction, low cost, and compact optics remain limited. We present a snapshot hyperspectral imaging system based on angular-to-spectral diversity conversion. A tapered kaleidoscope creates replicated views with distinct incidence directions, and a directly attached birefringent filter converts them into view-channel-dependent spectral transmittances, yielding complementary measurements that better condition the inverse problem for more stable single-shot spectral reconstruction. The system preserves a simple pixel-wise linear model for fast non-learning-based reconstruction and uses only off-the-shelf components without relay optics or cascaded modules. We select the birefringent filter configuration using a condition-number-based criterion and validate the system on both synthetic and real data.

</details>


### [107] [Reassessing Global Gradient-Norm Imbalance in BLIP Fine-Tuning Across Physical Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23655)
*Kiran Naseer,Samreen Azhar,Dwarikanath Mahapatra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉-语言模型中视觉与语言通路梯度范数失衡是否一定需要“校正”。跨三类物理域迁移（海底、航拍、放射影像）与多种微调设定测量语言对视觉的梯度范数比（参数归一化）。结果显示：失衡大小随域显著变化且无固定排序；简单降学习率即可显著降低失衡，并在BLEU上接近最优；分阶段冻结总能降低比值但从未最好；强制两路梯度等幅在不同数据集上分别拿到全场最佳与最差（在完全微调方法中），表明“平衡程度”与“达成路径”同等关键；梯度比下降并不稳定预示性能。此外，常用的BLIP+LoRA配置实际上未更新任何视觉参数，修正后三数据集BLEU-4均提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社区普遍将视觉-语言梯度失衡视为需要修复的缺陷，并提出多种自适应校正方法。然而失衡是否本质有害、以及非自适应（简单）策略的实际收益缺乏系统证据，尤其在跨域微调与图文描述任务上。作者动机是：量化失衡与性能的关系，评估常见非自适应校正（学习率、冻结、等幅约束）的有效性与稳健性，并检验这种关系是否随物理域迁移而变化。

Method: - 指标：测量语言→视觉梯度范数比（参数归一化）作为失衡度。
- 数据与设定：三类域迁移图文描述数据集（海底、航拍、放射影像），九种微调条件，三随机种子。
- 对比方法（排除自适应类如BalGrad/OGM/PMR/CGGM）：
  1) 降低全局学习率；
  2) 分阶段冻结不同模块；
  3) 强制两路梯度等幅（使视觉与语言梯度范数相等）；
  4) 调度仅控制以隔离冻结效应。
- 评价：BLEU（含BLEU-4），并分析梯度比与性能的相关性。
- 额外检查：常用BLIP+LoRA配置是否实际更新视觉参数。

Result: - 失衡度在不同域差异显著且无一致排序。
- 简单降学习率：显著降低梯度比，且在所有数据集上BLEU落后最佳方法仅数个点。
- 分阶段冻结：各域均降低比值，但从未排名第一；通过仅调度对照发现其效应在一个数据集可归因于冻结，本于其他两个不成立。
- 强制等幅：将逐参数失衡逼近零，但在不同数据集上分别成为最优和最差的完全微调方案，设置相同。
- 总结性发现：降低梯度比并不能一致提升跨域caption性能，“达到平衡的方式”与“平衡水平”同等重要。
- 次要发现：常用BLIP+LoRA设置未实际更新视觉参数；修正后三数据集BLEU-4均提升。

Conclusion: “梯度失衡”并非普遍性缺陷。其大小不稳定且与性能并非单调相关；简单、非自适应手段（例如降学习率）已能获得接近最优的稳健结果，而强制等幅可能在不同域产生两极化效果。实践上应：
- 不将梯度等幅作为默认目标；
- 更关注达到平衡的策略与训练动态（如学习率、冻结计划）；
- 审核LoRA等配置是否真正更新预期模块，避免“无效适配”。

Abstract: Imbalanced gradient magnitudes between the visual and language pathways of a vision-language model are often treated as a defect to be corrected. We test that premise for one family of correction, deliberately excluding adaptive, signal-driven schemes (e.g. BalGrad, OGM, PMR, CGGM), which are a mechanistically distinct class outside this study's scope. Measuring the language-to-visual gradient-norm ratio, reported in parameter-normalised form, across nine fine-tuning conditions, three seeds, and three captioning datasets spanning distinct physical domain shifts -- underwater, aerial, radiological -- we find imbalance magnitude varies markedly across domains with no predictable ordering. A plain learning-rate reduction cuts imbalance substantially and lands within a few BLEU points of the best method on every dataset. Staged freezing reduces the ratio on every domain yet never ranks first; a schedule-only control isolates freezing as the cause on one dataset but not the other two. Forcing the two gradient groups to equal magnitude drives per-parameter imbalance close to zero on every domain, yet is both the best result in the study and the worst placement among full fine-tuning methods, on different datasets, with identical settings. Reductions in gradient-norm ratio do not consistently predict captioning performance across domains, and how a given level of balance is reached matters as much as the level itself. As a secondary finding, a commonly reused LoRA configuration applied to BLIP silently adapts zero visual parameters; correcting it improves BLEU-4 on all three datasets.

</details>


### [108] [Why Do Video Diffusion Models Violate Physics? Unveiling the Flaws in Attention Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23658)
*Yueyan Li,Haibo Wang,Caixia Yuan,Xiaojie Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文通过可解释性研究发现视频扩散模型在早期去噪阶段就形成“运动规划”轨迹，但受RoPE导致的空间注意力过快衰减影响，轨迹易被锁死到不合物理的位置。作者提出在不同去噪步中按步数缩放RoPE频率的轻量改动，减少注意力衰减，提升物理一致性；在免训练与带训练设置下均验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频扩散模型虽具备高视觉质量，却常出现违背物理规律的运动与交互。现有缓解方法多依赖外部先验或专门数据集，缺乏对模型内部机制的系统理解与可泛化的结构性解决方案。作者动机是：从模型内部可解释性出发，定位导致物理不一致的根因，并提出无需重度数据或外部先验的结构改进。

Method: 1) 对文本到视频扩散模型开展首次“运动规划”可解释性研究：分析早期去噪阶段的运动轨迹形成过程。2) 结合跨注意力的轨迹模式与因果头分析，定位主导运动规划的注意力头子集。3) 自注意力层面分析RoPE的空间注意力衰减效应，发现其在早期导致候选区域注意力过快集中与锁死。4) 提出按去噪步数自适应缩放RoPE频率的轻量结构修改，减缓注意力衰减，鼓励探索更合理的时空候选区域。5) 通过免训练与带训练两类实验验证。

Result: - 证实“先形体，后细节”的生成规律，并量化早期跨注意力轨迹对运动规划的主导作用。- 发现RoPE引发的空间注意力过度衰减会抑制相邻帧的合理轨迹，触发物理失败模式。- 提出的步数自适应RoPE缩放显著降低物理不一致现象，提升运动连贯与物理常识指标；在无需额外训练和在少量训练微调两种设置下均有效。

Conclusion: 视频扩散模型的物理失真根源之一在于RoPE导致的过快空间注意力衰减，使早期去噪中的运动候选被错误锁定。通过在去噪过程中动态缩放RoPE频率，可缓解该问题并增强物理一致性。研究还提供了识别“运动规划头”的可解释性框架，为后续结构改造与训练策略提供依据。

Abstract: Despite impressive visual quality, state-of-the-art video diffusion models often generate content that violates real-world physical laws. While existing solutions rely on external priors or specialized data, we investigate the root cause by exploring the internal mechanisms of these models. Specifically, we present the first interpretability study on the ''motion planning'' process of text-to-video diffusion models, revealing how motion trajectories form during early denoising stages. Building upon the ''first shape, then details'' finding, we combine cross-attention trajectory patterns with causal head contributions to identify a specific subset of attention heads driving motion planning. Further, our self-attention analysis shows that Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) induces excessive spatial attention decay. This causes early candidate regions to prematurely lock into physically implausible positions, suppressing reasonable trajectories in adjacent frames and triggering generation failure modes. To address this fundamental flaw, we propose a lightweight architectural modification that scales the frequency of RoPE across different denoising steps. This strategy reduces excessive attention decay, helping the model explore better candidate regions to establish coherent physical motion. Finally, training-free and training-based experiments confirm the effectiveness of our approach in enhancing the physical commonsense of generated videos.

</details>


### [109] [Which Terrain Is Better? Preference Learning with VLM Prototypes for Off-Road Traversability Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23673)
*Ji-Hoon Hwang,Jisung Bae,E-In Son,Dong-Wook Kim,Jung-Taak Kim,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TravPro：将视觉可通行性从“打分”转为“排序”，用比较监督与VLM原型读出器生成稠密偏好伪标签，并蒸馏到RGB学生网络；在5个未见域上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统越野导航常用语义或可通行性分数，但缺乏统一真值，导致只能用每类固定值或自由空间置信度，无法表达“更偏好哪块地面”。作者将问题转化为“视觉可通行性排序”，以相对偏好比较来监督，避免绝对分数的主观性与域移问题。

Method: 1) 将标准标注（语义/可通行/障碍）转化为有序区域对，作为相对偏好监督；2) 冻结VLM，提取patch token并一次性聚类成固定原型库；3) 训练一个小读出头，为每个原型学习偏好得分；4) 将读出应用至所有patch，作为“教师”把稀疏比较扩展为稠密偏好伪标签；5) 训练RGB学生网络，蒸馏教师生成的偏好图，并联学习非地面掩码以排除障碍/背景；6) 评估在跨域场景上的排序准确率。

Result: 在5个未见域上，TravPro的成对准确率达0.915，显著优于最强基线的0.783；能够对地表条件（如湿滑、松散等）产生细粒度排序，超越按类别设定的固定分值。直接用同一VLM做提示并以稠密监督训练则无法得到有效排序，显示关键在于“如何使用”VLM与监督。

Conclusion: 相对偏好监督与VLM原型读出结合，可将稀疏比较转化为稠密偏好标签，从而学习到跨域泛化的地形偏好排序。TravPro无需像素级标注，优于基线且对地表细节敏感，提示未来越野导航应关注“排序学习”与教师-学生蒸馏的设计。

Abstract: In vision-based off-road navigation, a robot needs to know not only which obstacles to avoid but also which terrain is better. The first is handled by freespace detection or semantic segmentation. The second is usually answered with a traversability score, but no universal ground truth exists for such a score, so perception falls back on a predefined value per semantic class or a freespace confidence. These scores say what a region is, not which region a robot should prefer. We therefore formulate this preference as visual traversability ranking, an ordering of visible terrain that can be supervised by comparisons between two regions. Standard annotations do not label preference, but they imply its direction. We present TravPro, which converts these annotations into ordered region pairs and fits a small readout on frozen vision--language model (VLM) patch tokens to these pairs. The tokens are clustered once into a fixed prototype bank, and the readout learns a preference score per prototype. The readout is then applied to every patch and serves as a teacher that turns sparse comparisons into dense preference pseudo-labels without pixel-wise annotation. An RGB student distills these maps into a dense terrain-preference map together with a non-ground mask that excludes obstacles and background from the ranking. On five unseen domains, TravPro reaches a mean pairwise accuracy of 0.915 against 0.783 for the strongest baseline, producing an ordering sensitive to surface condition that a per-class value cannot represent. The same VLM and the same supervision yield no such ordering when the VLM is prompted and the supervision is used as dense targets; what matters is how they are used.

</details>


### [110] [Mind the Gaps: A Curated Benchmark for Form Field Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23679)
*Iheb Brini,Omar Moured,Hamza Gbada,Elisa Barney*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出mini-CommonForms，一个高质量一致标注的表单字段检测（FFD）基准，并系统评估SOTA检测方法，促进可复现研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FFD不同于常规目标检测，很多字段由版面结构与留白而非显性前景定义；现有大规模数据集多用启发式标注，噪声大且不一致，导致评估不可靠，需要一个精心策划、干净一致的基准数据集。

Method: 构建并人工校准一个小而精的FFD基准数据集mini-CommonForms，提供一致高质量标注；基于该基准对多种SOTA检测方法进行细致对比评测；公开数据与代码以支持可复现性与无障碍应用研究。

Result: 得到一个高质量、标注一致的FFD数据集与完整评测结果，显示SOTA方法在以结构与留白定义字段的场景存在挑战；基准与评估为后续方法改进提供了清晰对照。

Conclusion: mini-CommonForms填补了FFD基准中高质量一致标注的空缺，能更可靠地评估与推动文档自动化及可及性方向的研究；资源已开源以促进社区使用与复现。

Abstract: Form Field Detection (FFD) is a fundamental component of document understanding systems, enabling applications ranging from large-scale industrial digitization to accessible form interaction for automated analysis. Unlike conventional object detection tasks, FFD is inherently challenging because fields are often defined by layout structure and whitespace rather than visible foreground content. Existing large-scale datasets frequently rely on heuristic annotation pipelines, resulting in noisy and inconsistent labels that hinder reliable evaluation. In this work, we introduce mini-CommonForms, a carefully curated FFD benchmark with consistent, high-quality annotations, and present a detailed evaluation of state-of-the-art detection approaches. The benchmark is designed to support reproducible research in document automation and accessibility-oriented applications. Dataset and code are available at https://github.com/moured/mini-commonforms

</details>


### [111] [Infectious Bovine Pinkeye Detection Using Computer Vision and Imbalance-Aware Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23714)
*Michael Abalo,Jameson Brennan,Hossein Moradi Rekabdarkolaee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究比较YOLOv11与YOLOv26在牛传染性结膜炎（pinkeye）自动分类上的表现，并评估随机少数类过采样（RMO）与改进SMOTE的类平衡效果；多数配置达到了约0.99准确率与0.98宏F1，部分变体TPR达1.00且无漏检。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统诊断依赖人工临床观察，主观且耗时，难以在大群体或偏远环境中高效实施；需要自动化、客观、高效的计算机视觉方法以提升牛只健康监测与生产效益。

Method: 以YOLOv11与YOLOv26两种目标检测/分类架构的五种尺寸变体（n/s/m/l/x）为基线，在原始类别不平衡数据、RMO以及改进版SMOTE三种训练集设置下训练与评估；比较各变体在准确率、宏F1、TPR（召回）与是否出现漏检（FN）上的差异。

Result: 两系模型总体表现强。YOLOv11中，RMO-s达准确率0.99、宏F1 0.98、TPR 1.00且无FN；RMO-m同样TPR 1.00无FN。YOLOv26中，原始l、RMO-m与RMO-l均达准确率0.99与宏F1 0.98；其中RMO-l达TPR 1.00无FN。总体上，RMO较改进SMOTE更能提升少数类检测，而YOLOv26-l在原始数据上已很强，说明并非所有变体都需过采样。

Conclusion: YOLOv11与YOLOv26均适用于牛pinkeye的自动检测；RMO通常优于改进SMOTE以改善少数类召回，但对强基线（如YOLOv26-l）未必必需。建议在畜牧健康监测中进一步验证与部署，依据数据不平衡程度与模型变体选择是否过采样。

Abstract: Infectious bovine pinkeye is a contagious ocular disease that adversely affects cattle health, welfare, and agricultural productivity. Conventional diagnosis relies primarily on clinical observation, which can be subjective, time-consuming, and difficult to implement efficiently in large herds or remote settings. This study evaluated and compared You Only Look Once (YOLO) v11 and YOLOv26 for automated bovine pinkeye classification and investigated the effects of class-balancing strategies on model performance. Five variants (n, s, m, l, and x) of each architecture were trained and evaluated using the original imbalanced dataset, Random Minority Oversampling (RMO), and an adapted Synthetic Minority Oversampling Technique (SMOTE). Both YOLOv11 and YOLOv26 demonstrated strong classification performance, although the effects of class balancing varied across model variants. For YOLOv11, RMO-s achieved an accuracy of 0.99, a macro F1-score of 0.98, and a true positive rate (TPR) of 1.00, with no false-negative classifications. RMO-m also achieved a TPR of 1.00 with no false negatives. For YOLOv26, the original l, RMO-m, and RMO-l variants each achieved an accuracy of 0.99 and a macro F1-score of 0.98, with RMO-l attaining a TPR of 1.00 and no false negatives. Overall, RMO generally provided greater improvements in minority-class detection than adapted SMOTE, whereas the strong performance of the original YOLOv26-l demonstrates that oversampling was not necessary for all model variants. These findings demonstrate the potential of YOLOv11 and YOLOv26 for automated detection of bovine pinkeye and support further evaluation for livestock health monitoring.

</details>


### [112] [Layer-Aware Position Embeddings for Visual Token Pruning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23715)
*Yahong Wang,Zhangkai Ni,Juncheng Wu,Yuyin Zhou,Ying Wen,Lianghua He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种层感知位置编码策略，在MLLM剪枝时于关键层使用稀疏位置编码、其余层用连续位置编码，以兼顾感知与定位能力，显著提升剪枝后性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM对图像需数百视觉token，推理开销大。现有token剪枝需为保留token重置位置编码：稀疏编码降低对视觉token的注意力，损害感知；连续编码破坏空间对应，削弱grounding。需要一种既保留定位又维持整体性能的策略。

Method: 对语言解码器做逐层分析，发现中间层对grounding至关重要。据此提出层感知位置编码：在“grounding敏感层”采用稀疏位置编码（保持原稀疏离散索引、避免空间混淆），在其他层用连续位置编码（提升注意力与融合）。可与多种剪枝方法无缝结合。

Result: 在多种代表性剪枝方法与多样基准上进行实验，层感知策略在综合多模态指标上均优于纯稀疏或纯连续位置编码的基线，兼顾感知与定位能力。

Conclusion: 中间层是保持剪枝后MLLM定位能力的关键。通过按层切换位置编码类型，可在不增加额外计算的情况下显著提升剪枝后性能，为MLLM高效推理提供实用方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) incur substantial computational overhead due to the reliance on hundreds of visual tokens to represent images. While token pruning has emerged as a promising approach to reduce the inference cost of MLLMs, existing methods typically reassign position embeddings to the retained tokens using either sparse or continuous position embeddings, each introducing distinct limitations. Sparse position embeddings tend to decrease the attention value allocated to visual tokens, thereby degrading the perception capability of MLLMs, whereas continuous position embeddings disrupt the original spatial correspondence of visual tokens, leading to weakened grounding capability. To mitigate this issue, we perform layer-wise analysis of the language decoder and observe that intermediate layers play a critical role for maintaining the grounding capability of MLLMs under token pruning. Based on this observation, we propose a layer-aware position embedding strategy, which switches to sparse position embeddings at grounding-sensitive layers while maintaining continuous position embeddings elsewhere. Extensive experiments across representative pruning methods and diverse benchmarks demonstrate that our approach improves the comprehensive multimodal performance of pruned MLLMs compared with standard sparse and continuous position embeddings.

</details>


### [113] [BindCLIP: One Balanced Coupling For Compositional Vision Language Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23717)
*Liuyang Song,Yi Zhang,Zhongyi Deng,Daqian Yang,Hongbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BindCLIP在不微调编码器的前提下，用一个平衡的token-patch-depth最优传输耦合读出图文匹配中的词-区域-层级对应关系，形成反对称的成对打分器，显著提升对组合与关系的判断能力，优于冻结的全局CLIP，在多基准上提升并通过对照与消融验证其机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局图文相似度压缩为单一向量，会丢失“哪词对应哪区域、区域如何排列”的结构，导致模型可识别对象却偏好组合错误的描述。作者认为这类对应结构已隐含在冻结编码器中，关键是如何读取而非重建它。

Method: 提出BindCLIP：在冻结的CLIP表示上，构造一个平衡的token–patch–depth最优传输（OT）三方耦合，将候选文本与多层视觉特征放入同一运输计划中。通过该耦合的能量读出语义、实体一致、顺序与空间证据；交换候选会置换运输计划，从而得到严格反对称的成对评分。在耦合内加入几何细化，抑制缺乏文本与层级支持的传输移动。无需任务标签、解析器、关系库或检测器。

Result: 单一检查点与单一路径推理，在What’sUp、ARO、SugarCrepe上全面优于冻结的全局CLIP，尤其在关系拆分上迁移最强。控制实验排除“访问图像patch”与“字幕长度”捷径；推理时的病灶实验将空间编排能力定位到耦合模块。

Conclusion: 冻结编码器蕴含细粒度词-区域-层级结构；通过OT耦合读取而非重建这些结构，可在无需额外监督与组件的前提下显著提升组合与关系理解，并具有良好的可转移性与可解释性。

Abstract: Global vision--language similarities compress an image and a caption into one vector, preserving semantics but not which word corresponds to which region or how those regions are arranged; a model can recognize every word and object yet prefer a compositionally incorrect caption. We argue that a frozen encoder retains this association structure, so the problem is to read it rather than to rebuild it beside the pretrained similarity. We introduce BindCLIP, a pairwise scorer built on one latent object: a balanced token--patch--depth optimal-transport coupling that places both candidate captions and several visual depths in a single plan. Semantic, entity, order, and spatial evidence are read as energies of this state, and exchanging the candidates permutes the plan, making the score exactly antisymmetric. A geometric refinement inside the coupling contracts moves that the candidates and the visual depths do not support. No task label, parser, relation inventory, or detector is used. One checkpoint and one inference path improve the official What'sUp, ARO, and SugarCrepe benchmarks over frozen global CLIP, with the strongest transfer on the relation splits. Controls rule out patch access and caption-length shortcuts, and an inference-time lesion localizes spatial arrangement to the coupling.

</details>


### [114] [VGGT-Prime: Compute-Adaptive Mixture-of-Heads for Efficient Visual Geometry Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23733)
*Abteen Arab,Guile Wu,Chengjie Huang,Dongfeng Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出VGGT-Prime：一种针对多视图直接3D重建的视觉几何Transformer的“按需计算”混合注意力头架构，通过动态选择全局注意力层中各注意力头的计算模式，显著降低推理复杂度，在保持重建质量的同时实现最高8×加速，并与现有令牌稀疏/合并方法互补，最高可达14×加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VGGT等前馈视觉几何模型依赖全局注意力，计算与视图数二次增长，长序列推理延迟大。已有加速多聚焦于减少token冗余（合并或KV稀疏），但忽略了架构层面的冗余：多头注意力中并非所有头都携带关键几何信息，存在可被压缩/重分配的空间。

Method: 识别并利用多头注意力中的“架构冗余”。提出VGGT-Prime：在每个全局注意力层引入轻量级router，对每个注意力头估计所需计算等级，并将其动态分配到不同计算模式（例如全计算、降采样/近似、或跳过），形成compute-adaptive的mixture-of-heads机制，从而在不改变输入token的前提下降低总体计算。

Result: 在多个数据集上，相比VGGT实现约8×推理加速，同时在相机位姿、深度、点云预测等指标上保持有竞争力的精度。与token合并等现有方法结合，可进一步将总体加速提升到约14×。

Conclusion: 多视图视觉几何Transformer的性能瓶颈不仅在于token冗余，也存在显著的架构冗余。通过对注意力头进行动态、细粒度的计算分配，VGGT-Prime在几乎不损失重建质量的前提下显著加速推理，并且与现有加速技术互补，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Feed-forward visual geometry models such as the Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer (VGGT) have recently enabled direct 3D reconstruction from multi-view images. Despite their promising performance, these models scale quadratically with the number of input views due to their global attention mechanism, resulting in substantial latency for long sequence inputs. There have been some recent efforts to accelerate VGGT, but they primarily focus on reducing \emph{token redundancy} through token merging or key/value sparsification. Our work resolves this bottleneck from a different perspective by investigating \emph{architectural redundancy} in visual geometry transformers. We show that the multi-head attention modules in VGGT's global-attention layers contain substantial architectural redundancy, with only a subset of heads carrying critical geometric information. In light of this observation, we propose VGGT-Prime, a compute-adaptive mixture-of-heads model that resolves this redundancy to accelerate visual geometry transformers while maintaining competitive reconstruction quality. The key idea of VGGT-Prime is to estimate the appropriate computation level for each global-attention head using a lightweight router and then dynamically assign each head to different computation modes. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that VGGT-Prime can achieve an {$8\times$} inference speedup over VGGT while maintaining competitive performance on camera pose, depth, and point-cloud predictions. We further show that VGGT-Prime is complementary to existing acceleration methods, such as token merging, further improving inference speed by up to $14{\times}$ over VGGT. An overview of our work is available on our \href{https://vggt-prime.github.io}{project page}.

</details>


### [115] [OnlineWM: Causality-Aware Active Online Learning for Effective World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23753)
*Yikun Miao,Fangqi Zhu,Quanxin Shou,Xiaoyi Pang,Zhengyang Yan,Junhao Li,Haodong Wang,Zicong Hong,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OnlineWM 是一种在线训练的生成式世界模型框架，通过主动与模拟器交互获取高价值数据，并使用因果感知的反事实学习来提升对动作的可控性与因果归因，从而在多样场景和新域上更稳健、更泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型多依赖离线静态数据与最小化观测误差的目标，导致：1）训练数据分布与模型随时间演化的错误模式错配，长尾困难场景无法覆盖；2）目标函数易诱导模型依赖虚假相关，而非真实的动作-效果因果关系，削弱动作可控性与动态预测可靠性。

Method: 提出 OnlineWM：1）主动在线学习：模型根据当前预测薄弱点，自适应向模拟器发起查询，收集能最大化改进的交互序列；2）因果感知微调：在相同初始状态下对比不同动作的结果，采用反事实学习迫使模型将状态转移归因于具体动作，避免仅拟合环境背景演化。整体形成“主动数据采集—因果优化”的闭环迭代。

Result: 在大量实验中，OnlineWM 显著提高动作可控性，减小长尾与跨域分布偏移下的预测误差，并在未见过的领域上保持良好泛化。

Conclusion: 通过将主动数据获取与因果优化结合，OnlineWM 持续修正模型弱点并强化因果归因，构建更稳健的生成式世界模型，优于传统离线与仅最小化观测误差的方法。

Abstract: Generative world models aim to predict future states conditioned on actions, where action controllability is fundamental for reliable dynamics modeling. While recent efforts leverage simulator-generated data to enhance this capability, existing training pipelines face two fundamental limitations. First, static offline data collection leads to a distribution misalignment between training sets and the model's evolving error patterns, failing to resolve critical long-tail scenarios where dynamics predictions remain unreliable. Second, the standard objective of minimizing observational discrepancy often encourages the model to exploit spurious correlations instead of capturing the underlying action-effect causality. To address these limitations, we propose OnlineWM, an online training framework that continuously improves world modeling through active simulator interaction and causality-aware optimization. OnlineWM introduces two key innovations: (1) Active Online Learning: Instead of using fixed datasets, OnlineWM adaptively queries the simulator for new interaction sequences that target the model's current predictive weaknesses, ensuring high-utility data acquisition. (2) Causality-Aware Fine-Tuning: We propose a counterfactual learning strategy that contrasts the outcomes of different actions from identical states, forcing the model to attribute state transitions to specific actions rather than ambient environmental evolution, thereby grounding its predictions in reliable causal mechanisms. By integrating active data acquisition with causal optimization, OnlineWM establishes a closed-loop refinement process that ensures the model is both robust to diverse scenarios and precise in its causal attribution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OnlineWM significantly enhances action controllability and generalizes effectively to unseen domains.

</details>


### [116] [Training-Free Spectral Transductive Refinement for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23758)
*Fahim Rahman,S. M. Tanjeeb Meheran Rohan,Md. Taimum Ibne Sayed,Asaduzzaman Herok,Md. Bakhtiar Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种测试时无训练的跨域小样本识别方法STR，在冻结特征上通过谱坐标与伪标签迭代精炼，显著提升尤其是1-shot性能，且与重训练方法竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小样本识别在域移与一张标注图像（1-shot）下极其脆弱，单个支持样本难以可靠代表类别。作者想在不重训编码器、不过度增强源域的前提下，仅在测试时降低这种脆弱性。

Method: 在冻结视觉嵌入上，构建支持集与查询集的联合kNN图；计算归一化拉普拉斯的谱坐标，将整场景（episode）嵌入到谱空间；用带标签的支持样本初始化类别代表（原型）；对未标注查询进行伪标签并迭代更新类别代表进行“谱域精炼”。方法完全在推理时进行、为传导式设置。

Result: 在受控组件实验中（ResNet-18冻结特征、五个移位域），谱精炼在1-shot和5-shot上均超越单原型的谱初始化，1-shot提升最大。与CD-FSL最新方法对比（miniImageNet上预训ResNet-10，八个目标域），STR在1-shot平均精度最高，5-shot仍具竞争力，逼近依赖重度元训练与数据增强的方法。

Conclusion: 仅在测试阶段进行的谱传导精炼能显著缓解跨域小样本中的1-shot脆弱性；性能提升主要来自谱空间中的迭代精炼而非增加原型容量。方法简单、无训练、强适应性，但为传导设定，应明确其假设与适用场景。

Abstract: Few-shot recognition with frozen visual features is especially fragile under domain shift and one-shot supervision, where a single labelled image is an unreliable estimate of its class. We ask how far this fragility can be reduced purely at test time, without retraining the encoder or augmenting the source domain. We present Spectral Transductive Refinement (STR), a training-free transductive inference rule that exploits the geometry of the complete support-query episode. Given frozen embeddings, STR builds a joint k-nearest-neighbour graph, maps the episode into a normalized-Laplacian spectral coordinate system, initializes class representatives from the labelled support, and iteratively refines them using pseudo-labelled queries. We evaluate STR under two protocols. A controlled component study with frozen ResNet-18 features shows that spectral refinement consistently improves over single-prototype spectral initialization across five shifted domains, with the largest gains in the one-shot regime where support estimates are weakest. We then benchmark STR against recent Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning (CD-FSL) methods using the standard miniImageNet-pretrained ResNet-10 backbone over eight established target domains. Operating entirely at inference time, STR attains the highest 1-shot average among compared methods and remains competitive at 5-shot, rivalling approaches relying on heavy source-domain meta-training augmentations. Because STR is transductive, we report its setting explicitly. Diagnostics attribute its gains to iterative refinement in spectral coordinates rather than added prototype capacity, which remains inactive in our configuration.

</details>


### [117] [PRISM-RAG: Multimodal Hypergraph Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Tobacco Product and Legislative Policy Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23769)
*Manuel Serna-Aguilera,Raegan Anderes,Page Dobbs,Khoa Luu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NicoPRISM数据集与PRISM-RAG多模态超图RAG，解决跨法域相似法规文本导致的检索与推理混淆；在尼古丁产品合规与知识问答上显著提升按法域正确检索与合规回答表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG在面对不同法域间语义极其相似的法规文本时，易将生成模型引向“主题相关但法域错误”的证据，尤其尼古丁/烟草新型产品利用定义模糊规避监管，需能够识别“哪一法域的法律适用”而非仅检索相关文本；多模态场景下还需把图像属性与立法文本正确关联。

Method: 1) 构建NicoPRISM：包含161,563张产品图像、属性描述，覆盖13个美国法域的产品/健康/立法知识库，以及1,495经验证问答，涵盖政策合规与产品知识两类任务。2) 提出PRISM-RAG：以图像、属性字幕与实体为节点的多模态超图RAG；索引阶段零LLM调用；查询以产品图像为锚，利用法域感知的上下文组装机制，保证目标法域的法规文本必达LLM输入；查询时仅一次LLM调用。

Result: 在政策合规查询中，PRISM-RAG从正确法域检索段落的比例为93.9%，较标准RAG提升48.6个百分点（p<0.001）；在关键词、语义、法域正确率与合规准确度等多项指标上与SOTA持平或更优，同时保持极低LLM调用成本（索引0次、查询1次）。

Conclusion: 通过法域感知的多模态超图检索与新数据集支撑，能有效化解跨法域语义相似导致的上下文冲突，稳健连接产品图像属性与对应法域法规，显著提升尼古丁产品合规与知识问答的可靠性与可扩展性。

Abstract: The disambiguation of semantically similar statutory text across jurisdictions is a retrieval problem that existing methods do not solve. This inter-context conflict can steer generative models toward confidently produced answers grounded in topically relevant but jurisdictionally incorrect sources. Tobacco and nicotine regulations vary by US jurisdiction, often sharing similar language, thus, robust reasoning requires identifying which jurisdiction's law governs a given product, not merely retrieving relevant text. Emerging products (e.g., pouches) exploit ambiguous definitions to evade regulation. State-of-the-art (SOTA) document retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods struggle to address this inter-context conflict, and thus struggle to connect image attributes (e.g., rich attribute captions) to the set of similar legislation texts. We introduce NicoPRISM (Nicotine Product and Regulation Image-and-Text Surveillance Multimodal), comprising 161,563 images, attribute captions, a knowledge base of product, health, and legislative documents spanning 13 US jurisdictions, and 1,495 validated question-answer pairs across two tasks: policy compliance QA and product knowledge QA. We also propose PRISM-RAG, a multimodal hypergraph RAG framework built over images, captions, and entities without any LLM calls at index time, grounding every query in a product image and routes retrieval through a jurisdiction-aware context assembly mechanism guaranteeing that statutory text from the queried jurisdiction reaches the language model by construction. PRISM-RAG retrieves passages from the correct jurisdiction in 93.9% of policy compliance queries, a 48.6 percentage point advantage over standard RAG (p<0.001), using zero LLM calls at index time and one at query time, and is competitive with or outperforms SOTA RAG frameworks across keyword, semantic, jurisdiction-, and compliance-accuracy metrics.

</details>


### [118] [Mira-Scene: Pixel-Aligned Layouts for Generative 3D Scene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23796)
*Yang-Tian Sun,Tianjia Liu,Zehuan Huang,Yi-Hua Huang,Xiaoyang Lyu,Ziyi Yang,Zi-Xin Zou,Yuan-Chen Guo,Yan-Pei Cao,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mira-Scene以致密对应替代稀疏位姿回归，在单张图像条件下高保真生成物体并精准布局到3D场景中；通过CCM+PCM恢复物体变换，并用多模态扩散Transformer联合生成几何与CCM，显著提升布局精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有整体式方法牺牲物体细节，组合式方法虽保留细节但以稀疏、无界的姿态参数表示布局，难学且在缺少场景级标注时泛化差。需要一种既能利用可扩展的物体级数据、又能稳健推断场景布局的表示与方法。

Method: 提出Canonical Coordinate Map（CCM）：像素对齐、映射到物体有界规范空间的表面坐标场；结合由单目几何估计得到的场景点云图（PCM），形成致密的规范—场景对应，通过鲁棒几何对齐恢复物体位姿与尺度。引入多模态扩散Transformer，设立几何与CCM的专家分支，使用共享注意力与位置编码，联合生成物体几何与CCM以保证几何—布局一致性。CCM在有界空间中训练，可仅用可扩展物体级3D数据而不需场景级布局标注。

Result: 在室内、室外、合成与野外场景中，较强基线（如SAM3D）显著提升布局精度：3D-IoU相对提升39.8%，2D-IoU相对提升16.5%；在有限的开源训练数据条件下仍具优势。

Conclusion: 以致密、界定良好的CCM替代稀疏姿态回归，可在无需场景级标注的前提下稳健恢复单图3D场景布局；与多模态扩散Transformer的联合生成进一步保证几何与布局一致，显著提升单图3D场景重建的可用性与精度。

Abstract: Single-image 3D object generation can now produce high-fidelity assets, yet accurately placing them into a coherent scene layout remains an open challenge. A central difficulty lies in how object layout is represented. Holistic methods absorb placement into a scene-level generation process, sacrificing object-level detail. Compositional methods preserve object fidelity by decoupling geometry from layout, but typically parameterize layout as sparse, unbounded pose variables that are difficult to learn and generalize poorly under scarce scene-level supervision.We present Mira-Scene, a compositional 3D scene reconstruction framework that replaces sparse pose regression with dense, bounded correspondence recovery. At its core is the Canonical Coordinate Map (CCM), a pixel-aligned field that maps each visible object pixel to a surface coordinate in the object's bounded canonical space. When paired with a scene-space Point Cloud Map (PCM) from monocular geometry estimation, CCM induces dense canonical-to-scene correspondences from which object transformations are recovered through robust geometric alignment. Because CCM operates in bounded canonical space, it provides a stable prediction target that can be trained from scalable object-level 3D data without requiring scene-level layout annotations. Mira-Scene further introduces a multimodal diffusion transformer that jointly generates object geometry and CCMs, using modality-specific expert streams with shared attention and positional encoding to promote geometry-layout consistency. Experiments on indoor, outdoor, synthetic, and in-the-wild scenes show that Mira-Scene substantially outperforms strong baselines in layout accuracy, achieving relative gains of 39.8% in 3D-IoU and 16.5% in 2D-IoU over SAM3D, using limited open-source training data.

</details>


### [119] [Confidence-Aware Teacher-Student Distillation for 3D Medical Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23815)
*Georgios Triantafyllou,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种仅需每个体数据在单个2D切片上少量点提示即可进行全3D分割的学生-教师框架：教师用基础模型离线生成带置信度的体素伪标注，学生用置信度感知优化训练，忽略不可靠区域、强调高置信区域；在心脏MRI上显著超越半监督SOTA，减少标注负担并提升边界精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D医学分割依赖密集体素级标注，跨任务/模态难扩展；如何在极端稀疏标注（仅少量点）下仍能重建完整3D解剖结构，且避免伪标注噪声带来的误差传播，是亟需解决的问题。

Method: - 架构：离线教师（foundation model）+ 任务特定3D学生。
- 输入标注：每卷仅在一个2D切片提供少量正样本点提示。
- 教师阶段：利用点提示生成全体积伪标注，并为每体素估计空间置信度。
- 学生训练：采用置信度感知优化——在损失中静态屏蔽低置信区域、加权强调高置信区域，从而缓解噪声伪标注的负迁移。
- 评估：在3D心脏MRI数据集上与半监督SOTA比较。

Result: - 对半监督SOTA的分割性能提升最高达43.6%。
- 相比教师模型，表面边界精度提升最高14.7%。
- 仅需每卷少量正点提示，大幅降低人工标注成本。
- 向全监督上限收敛，弥补最高34.1%的性能差距。

Conclusion: 以极少点提示驱动的学生-教师框架可在3D医学分割中有效利用离线伪标注与置信度，引导学生专注可靠区域，显著提升性能与边界质量并降低标注开销，为跨任务/模态的可扩展分割提供实用途径。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation models typically rely on large amounts of densely annotated volumetric data, limiting their scalability across tasks and imaging modalities. This work addresses the challenge of predicting entire 3D anatomical structures from extreme annotation sparsity. An annotation-efficient student-teacher framework is proposed for automatic 3D medical segmentation that requires only a set of point prompts on a single 2D slice per volume, as input. A foundation model serves as an offline teacher, utilizing the provided point prompts from the selected slice to full-volume pseudo-annotations alongside their corresponding spatial confidence scores prior to student training. To mitigate the error propagation of noisy pseudo-annotations, a task-specific 3D student network is trained using a confidence-aware optimization strategy. By leveraging the teacher's pre-computed confidence scores, this strategy explicitly excludes statically uncertain regions of the pseudo-annotations from the loss calculation, while simultaneously emphasizing regions with higher confidence. Evaluated on 3D cardiac MRI datasets, our framework outperforms state-of-the-art semi-supervised methods, improving segmentation performance by up to 43.6%. Furthermore, it drastically reduces the manual annotation burden to just a few positive point prompts per volume, while improving surface boundary precision by up to 14.7% over the teacher and successfully recovering up to 34.1% of the performance gap toward the fully supervised upper bound.

</details>


### [120] [VISTA: Video-Injected Stylized Text-to-Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23817)
*Monseej Purkayastha,Anindita Ghosh,Philipp Slusallek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISTA 是一个两阶段框架，将文本描述的结构性内容与参考视频的风格表达相融合，以生成风格化的3D人体动作，无需成对的（文本、视频、风格化动作）三元组数据。它通过双通道自编码器构建共享潜空间，并在其中用掩码自回归扩散和晚期融合的Dual-AdaLN注入视频风格，同时保持文本内容结构；跨批次无配对训练与潜空间循环一致性实现跨数据集联合学习，推理时可用三路无分类器指导独立调节内容与风格平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频条件或文本条件的3D动作生成难以同时控制“内容”（做什么）与“风格”（怎么做），且通常需要昂贵的成对注释数据。作者希望在无需三元配对数据的情况下，实现从文本获得语义结构、从视频获得风格表达的可控融合，并在统一模型中学习跨语义丰富与风格多样的数据。

Method: 两阶段：1）双通道自编码器将动作序列与视频片段编码到共享潜在流形，实现跨模态对齐。2）在潜空间中用掩码自回归扩散主干生成，采用专门的晚期融合Dual-AdaLN通道注入视频风格，同时以文本条件保持内容结构。训练上采用跨批次无配对策略，并通过潜空间循环一致性约束联合学习语义与风格。推理时使用分解的三路无分类器指导，实现对内容与风格权衡的可控调节。

Result: 在以渲染的动作捕捉为参考视频的验证中，相比其他视频条件方法，VISTA获得最高的风格识别准确率，同时内容对齐指标保持有竞争力；其三路无分类器指导在推理中可独立、可控地调节内容与风格的平衡。

Conclusion: VISTA证明了无需三元配对数据即可在共享潜空间中实现文本内容与视频风格的解耦与融合，达到优异的风格保持并兼顾内容一致性，且提供实用的可控推理机制，展示了在可控动画合成方面的潜力。

Abstract: We present VISTA, a two-stage framework for generating stylized 3D human motion by fusing structural content from text prompts with expressive style from reference videos, without requiring jointly paired (text, video, stylized motion) triplets. A Dual-channel Autoencoder first maps motion sequences and video clips into a shared latent manifold. A masked autoregressive diffusion backbone then operates within this manifold, injecting video-derived style through a dedicated late-fusion Dual-AdaLN pathway while preserving text-conditioned content structure. A cross-batch unpaired training protocol with latent cycle consistency enables joint learning across separate semantically rich and stylistically diverse datasets. As a proof-of-concept for controllable animation synthesis, we validate VISTA on rendered motion-capture references: it achieves the highest style recognition accuracy among video-conditioned methods while preserving competitive content alignment, and its decomposed 3-way classifier-free guidance provides independent, user-controllable calibration of the content--style balance at inference time.

</details>


### [121] [DFD-Lab: A Modular Audio-Visual Deepfake Detection Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23830)
*Jan Rybarczyk,Mateusz Roszkowski,Jacek Komorowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 他们提出DFD-Lab：一个模块化的音频-视频深伪检测实验管线，解耦数据适配、时序表示、模型接口与实验条件，支持共享训练与评估；通过三种检测器与多种训练/评测设置展示其必要性与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对音视频深伪检测器的比较混杂：数据集间迁移、时序建模、模型接口差异、训练增强与评测扰动常被混为一谈，导致结论不稳与不可复现。需要一个可模块化、可复用、可配置的统一管线来隔离这些因素，公平比较并复现实验。

Method: 提出DFD-Lab模块化管线，分离数据集适配、时序输入表示、模型接口与实验配置；集成三种实现：1) 基于Xception的最大logit融合；2) ResNet + 时序LSTM融合；3) 重新实现的AVFF。设计统一训练/评测流程，开展：跨数据集外测、基于退化的训练增强（如JPEG50）、以及评测时腐蚀（corruption）实验；在Deepfake-Eval-2024过滤子集上评估，训练集为FakeAVCeleb。

Result: 在未增强训练下（训练集FakeAVCeleb，对Deepfake-Eval-2024子集外测），三模型AUROC分别为0.504、0.538、0.458，接近随机或较差；加入JPEG50训练增强后AUROC提升至0.691、0.605、0.570，但分类准确率均下降；评测时的腐蚀也影响结果，显示指标对干预与腐蚀敏感且不一致。

Conclusion: 应在统一管线内将训练干预、评测腐蚀与度量选择明确区分，以获得可解释与可复现的比较；DFD-Lab提供可交换的音视频处理、检测器与可配置实验工作流，并通过实证展示跨数据集检测的困难以及排序型指标与分类指标提供的互补信息。

Abstract: Comparing audio-visual deepfake detectors requires coordinating dataset adaptation, temporal input representation, model interfaces and experimental conditions. We present DFD-Lab, a modular pipeline that separates these responsibilities while supporting shared training and evaluation workflows. We integrate three implementations: Xception-based maximum-logit fusion, ResNet with temporal LSTM fusion, and our AVFF reimplementation. Experiments cover external testing, degradation-based training augmentation and evaluation-time corruption. On a filtered subset of Deepfake-Eval-2024, models trained on FakeAVCeleb attain baseline AUROC values of 0.504, 0.538 and 0.458. JPEG50 training augmentation raises these to 0.691, 0.605 and 0.570, respectively, while all three accuracies decrease. These results illustrate why training interventions, evaluation corruptions and metric-dependent outcomes should remain distinct within a common pipeline. The contribution is the integration of audio-visual processing, interchangeable detectors and configurable experimental workflows, supported by empirical case studies. The findings highlight the challenge of cross-dataset detection and the complementary information provided by ranking and classification metrics.

</details>


### [122] [Comparative Performance and Parameter-Efficient Adaptation of DINOv2 for Active Trachoma Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23832)
*Kibrom Gebremedhin,Hadush Hailu,Bruk Gebregziabher,Yordanos Hailu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究对沙眼结膜照片的自动分级进行受控评估：在UCSF/Lietman 1,546张图像上，用OPTED做零样本结膜分割与标准化，比较多种预训练骨干与轻量适配，并在DINOv2上结合ECA与不同损失；最佳方案在五折下达成约91.7%准确率、90.7%宏F1、96.1% AUC，且ECA仅增5个参数，但其收益依赖训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低沙眼流行病学调查的人力成本与评分主观性；澄清在统一协议下，现代预训练表征、轻量适配模块与训练目标三者的相对贡献与权衡。

Method: - 数据：UCSF/Lietman公开1,546张结膜图像；二分类（TF vs 正常）。
- 预处理：OPTED流水线实现零样本睑板结膜分割、对齐、裁剪、标准化。
- 模型：先用统一分类头对比6个预训练骨干；再在DINOv2 ViT-B/14上评估4种轻量适配（含ECA）。
- 训练/评估：分层五折交叉验证；比较不同损失设计（交叉熵、focal、center及其组合），并做客观消融。

Result: 最佳：DINOv2+ECA+focal+center loss在五折下达成Accuracy 91.66±0.97%、Macro-F1 90.69±1.10%、AUC 96.06±0.71%。ECA仅增加5个可学习参数，性能可与更大替代方案匹配。消融显示ECA并非对所有损失都稳定增益；用交叉熵+center loss得到方差最低且同为91.66%的准确率。总体上，精调后的DINOv2贡献了主要性能，ECA提供了与训练目标相关的参数高效微调。

Conclusion: 在统一、可复现的流程下，DINOv2表征在沙眼TF影像二分类中占主导；ECA作为极轻量的通道注意力可在合适损失下带来额外但依赖性的增益。该工作给出可复现实验基线与基准，为后续在主动性沙眼影像分类上的方法比较提供参考。

Abstract: Automated grading of conjunctival photographs could reduce the cost and variability of trachoma prevalence surveys, but the relative value of modern pretrained visual representations, lightweight feature adaptation, and training-objective design has not been established under a common protocol. This study presents a controlled evaluation for binary classification of Trachomatous Inflammation-Follicular (TF) versus Normal using 1,546 images from the public UCSF/Lietman collection. Images are processed using the OPTED pipeline for zero-shot tarsal-conjunctiva segmentation, alignment, cropping, and standardization. We first compare six pretrained backbones using a common classification pipeline and then evaluate four lightweight adaptation mechanisms on DINOv2 ViT-B/14. Under stratified five-fold cross-validation, DINOv2 with Efficient Channel Attention (ECA) and focal-plus-center loss achieved 91.66 +/- 0.97% accuracy, 90.69 +/- 1.10% macro-F1, and 96.06 +/- 0.71% AUC. ECA introduces only five learnable parameters while matching the performance of substantially larger alternatives. Objective ablation further showed that ECA did not consistently improve plain DINOv2 across loss functions; the lowest-variance 91.66% accuracy was obtained with cross-entropy plus center loss. Overall, the fine-tuned DINOv2 representation provided most of the predictive performance, while ECA offered a highly parameter-efficient refinement whose effect depended on the training objective. The resulting workflow provides a reproducible benchmark for active trachoma image classification.

</details>


### [123] [MotionJEPA: Preventing Temporal Feature Collapse by Capturing Visual Changes in Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23881)
*Markus Karmann,Shile Li,Christian Internò,Bruno Andreis,David Klindt,Randall Balestriero,Jindong Gu,Philip Torr,Qi Zhang,Peng-Tao Jiang,Hao Zhang,Bo Li,Onay Urfalioglu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DISReg正则并整合到JEPA形成MotionJEPA，通过预测差分图像嵌入在不做重建的前提下平衡静态与动态特征，缓解慢特征偏置与表征塌缩，得到更完整且几何更简单的潜在表征，并在含静态干扰背景的多环境规划任务上提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准JEPA偏向“慢特征”，易抑制动态信息并导致潜在表征塌缩；现有用逆动力学抗塌缩需动作标签且难覆盖无标签的通用动态。因此需要一种无需像素重建、又能同时鼓励静态与动态特征的正则化方法，以获得任务无关、完整且稳定的世界模型表征。

Method: 提出DISReg：在JEPA中加入一个不依赖像素重建的逆动力学式模块，预测“差分图像嵌入”。正则由两部分组成：(1) 静态项：塑形图像嵌入分布，鼓励慢特征；(2) 动态项：不直接约束嵌入形状/分布，仅要求存在可预测的动态特征。将DISReg整合为MotionJEPA，并通过潜在探测与轨迹几何分析评估。

Result: MotionJEPA学到的表示更“完整”，在潜在探测任务中覆盖静态与动态因素；其潜在轨迹具有低曲率、几何更简单；在四个含静态背景干扰的环境中，下游规划成功率显著提升，相比基线更稳健。

Conclusion: 通过DISReg在JEPA中平衡静与动的信息，MotionJEPA缓解慢特征偏置与表示塌缩，在不依赖重建与动作标签的前提下获得结构简单而表达力强的潜在表示，从而提升含干扰环境中的下游规划性能。

Abstract: Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) are a promising paradigm for learning task-agnostic latent world models without visual reconstruction. However, standard JEPA training exhibits a strong inductive bias towards slow features, causing feature suppression and the collapse of latent representation. While inverse dynamics provides temporal anti-collapse, it relies on action labels and offers little incentive to embed general, unlabeled dynamics. We introduce Difference Image and Single image embedding Regularization (DISReg), a novel regularizer that builds on an inverse-dynamics-style module that predicts temporal difference image embeddings without any pixel reconstruction loss, encouraging balanced static and dynamic feature learning. DISReg consists of a static term that shapes the distribution of the image embedding and encourages slow features, and a dynamic term, which, unlike direct regularization on the embedding, imposes no constraint on the image embedding's shape or distribution and instead only incentivizes that dynamic features be present. By integrating this regularizer into a standard JEPA, we establish our new architecture, MotionJEPA. Latent probing demonstrates that MotionJEPA produces more complete representations than other methods, and our trajectory analysis shows it maintains geometrically simple latent embeddings with low curvature. We further show that MotionJEPA improves downstream planning success under static-background distractors across four environments.

</details>


### [124] [Colon3R: Cross-Domain 3D Reconstruction from Monocular Colonoscopic Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23961)
*Zhihao Xing,Yingyu Wang,Liang Zhao,Shoudong Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Colon3R：一种基于VGGT的跨域半监督框架，将带标注的仿真/体模几何知识迁移到无标注的体内结肠镜视频，实现更稳健的深度、点云与相机位姿重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目结肠镜3D重建因弱纹理、高反光、视野重叠少与组织非刚性而困难；传统多视几何假设在临床常被破坏，且临床标注稀缺，限制了几何基础模型在真实结肠镜上的适配。

Method: 以预训练VGGT为基础，提出Colon3R跨域半监督学习：1) 教师-学生框架，利用无标注体内视频的跨视角监督；2) 分层“准刚性”可靠性选择（序列级、定向帧对级、像素级）筛选可信监督；3) 源域保持式适配，避免在目标域适配时遗忘已学到的耦合几何（相机、深度、点图）。

Result: 在深度、点图与相机位姿估计上全面优于SOTA；在真实体内结肠镜上展示更完整且几何一致的重建，能更好应对临床域偏移。

Conclusion: 不依赖目标域几何标注的跨域半监督策略，可稳健迁移几何能力至体内结肠镜，显著提升单目重建质量；代码将在论文接收后开源。

Abstract: Monocular colonoscopic 3D reconstruction is important for surgical robotic colonoscopy, but remains challenging due to weak texture, specular reflections, limited view overlap, and non-rigid tissue motion. Conventional multi-view 3D reconstruction methods rely on stable correspondences and approximate rigidity, which are often violated in colonoscopy. Existing endoscopic methods often rely on domain-specific supervision, whereas there are not enough in-vivo labeled data available to adapt geometry foundation models to clinical colonoscopy. We present Colon3R, a cross-domain semi-supervised framework built on pretrained VGGT that transfers coupled camera, depth, and pointmap geometry from labeled phantom and simulated data to unlabeled in-vivo colonoscopy without requiring target-domain geometric annotations. Unlike source-only fine-tuning, which learns only from phantom and simulated data, Colon3R directly exploits unlabeled in-vivo video through teacher-derived cross-view supervision. Our proposed hierarchical quasi-rigid reliability selects reliable supervision at the sequence, directed-pair, and pixel levels, while source-preserving adaptation retains the learned coupled geometry during target-domain adaptation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior overall performance over state-of-the-art approaches in depth, pointmap, and camera pose estimation. Qualitative comparisons on real in-vivo colonoscopy further show substantially more complete and geometrically consistent reconstructions than competing methods under clinical domain shift. The code will be public available after the paper is accepted.

</details>


### [125] [Rethinking Diffusion Segmentation: When Does It Rely on Its Noisy State, and Does Diffusion Matter?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23967)
*Hengzhuo Yang,Yuming Zeng,Yuling Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文审查扩散模型在全监督分割中的“噪声状态”是否真的被利用、以及是否带来确定性性能优势。结果显示：当训练监督路径必须经过“噪声→掩膜”重建时，各方法对扩散状态表现出依赖；而若存在绕过（直接分割监督不经噪声重建），性能可在破坏状态/错配时保持不变，显示未依赖该状态。与匹配的仅图像模型相比，多数场景下扩散并无确定性终点优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管扩散模型被广泛用于条件预测（如分割），但在全监督分割中，输入图像本身已足以直接预测目标。因此：1) 模型是否真正利用了扩散“状态”（噪声演化的中间表征）并不明确；2) 即便利用，该计算是否较“仅图像”基线有确定性优势亦未被证实。论文动机是建立审计方法来判别“状态依赖性”和“扩散特有计算”的实际价值。

Method: 对12个已发表的条件扩散分割方法、3个数据集、每种设置10个随机种子：1) 状态依赖性审计：在再训练中有意破坏目标衍生的状态内容或打乱图像-状态配对，观察性能变化；区分两类监督路径：a) 下游仍需从噪声量重建掩膜（无旁路）；b) 存在分割监督旁路（监督不必经噪声→掩膜重建）。并对5个原本可旁路的方法强制其监督经噪声重建，观察依赖性变化。2) 确定性效用评估：为每种扩散方法构建匹配的“仅图像”对照模型，比较终点分割性能。

Result: - 在40个“无旁路、需从噪声重建”的比较中，全部显示对扩散状态有依赖（破坏/错配会降性能）。- 在30个“有旁路”的比较中，性能保持参考水平，表明未依赖状态。- 将5个可旁路方法改为必须经噪声→掩膜重建后，相应30个比较全部从“性能保持”转为“状态依赖”。- 与匹配的仅图像模型对比：在35种设置中，28种仅图像模型达到相当或更好性能；其中20种原生方法对审计的两类状态属性均有依赖，仍有16种被仅图像模型匹配或超越。

Conclusion: 监督路径决定了审计到的“状态依赖性”：若训练/推理必须经噪声重建，模型更可能依赖扩散状态；若存在绕过，则往往不依赖。就确定性终点性能而言，匹配的仅图像模型常与之相当或更优，说明扩散特有计算并不必然带来优势。对于已具强条件信号的任务（如全监督分割），声称扩散带来增益需额外证据：证明模型确实使用了噪声状态，且扩散计算较“仅条件”对照带来可验证的改进。

Abstract: Diffusion models are increasingly adapted from generation to conditional prediction, where a conditioning signal is combined with an evolving noisy representation of the target. In fully supervised segmentation, however, the conditioning image can already support direct target prediction, so endpoint performance alone establishes neither reliance on the added diffusion state nor a deterministic advantage over image-only prediction. For state reliance, we disrupt target-derived state content or correct image-state pairing during retraining of twelve published methods across three datasets, with ten matched seeds per setting. All 40 original-method comparisons whose evaluated-mask routes remained downstream of noised-quantity reconstruction exhibited state reliance, whereas all 30 comparisons with a segmentation-supervised bypass preserved reference performance. Rerouting five originally bypass-capable methods by forcing segmentation supervision through noise-to-mask reconstruction converted all 30 corresponding comparisons from preserved performance to state reliance. For deterministic utility, matched image-only counterparts achieved similar or better performance in 28 of 35 settings overall, including 16 of 20 whose native methods relied on both audited state properties. These results identify supervision path as a determinant of state reliance in the audited methods. Separately, matched image-only counterparts show that diffusion-specific computation often provides no deterministic endpoint advantage, including in methods that rely on the audited state properties. More generally, when conditioning already supports strong target prediction, diffusion-specific claims require additional evidence that the added state is used and that diffusion-specific computation improves the claimed capability beyond a matched condition-only counterpart.

</details>


### [126] [Evaluating the Generalization of Neuroimaging Foundation Models on African Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23983)
*Oluwatobi Iyanuoluwa Akinmuleya,Olatokun Shamsudeen Akano,Samuel Danquah Ankapong,Olamide Lawal,Toufiq Musah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估4个预训练神经影像基础模型在尼日利亚小样本临床脑MRI上的三分类任务表现，发现冻结骨干几乎退化为多数类预测；端到端训练的ViT3D明显更优但总体仍有限，提示需进行参数高效适配与更广泛外部验证以实现公平部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前神经影像基础模型多在大型西方人群上预训练，泛化到代表性不足的非西方、小样本临床队列的能力不明；需要实证检验其在细粒度诊断分类中的有效性与公平性。

Method: 在尼日利亚临床MRI数据集（88例）上进行三分类（对照、痴呆、帕金森），比较四种基础模型（BrainIAC、Neuro-JEPA、NeuroVFM、Primus）作为冻结骨干在四种模态配置（T1w、T2w、T1w+T2w、FLAIR）下的表现，并与端到端训练的ViT3D基线对照；采用准确率与MCC及召回评估。

Result: 冻结的基础模型大多崩溃为多数类预测；仅Neuro-JEPA在FLAIR上有轻微区分力。端到端训练的ViT3D在所有任务上取得更高的准确率与MCC（最高53.4%准确率，MCC=0.27），且是唯一具有非平凡召回的模型。

Conclusion: 以西方数据预训练且冻结的神经影像基础模型难以在小型、非西方临床队列中完成细粒度诊断分类；应采用参数高效微调等适配策略，并开展跨站点外部验证，以实现更公平的全球健康部署。

Abstract: Neuroimaging foundation models pretrained on large, predominantly western cohorts are increasingly proposed as general-purpose backbones for brain MRI analysis. Yet, their ability to generalize to underrepresented clinical populations remains largely untested. We evaluate four recent foundation models (BrainIAC, Neuro-JEPA, NeuroVFM, and Primus) on a three-way diagnostic classification task (Control, Dementia, Parkinson's disease) using a cohort of 88 subjects from a Nigerian clinical brain MRI dataset, across four modality configurations (T1w, T2w, T1w+T2w, FLAIR), and compare against an end-to-end trained ViT3D baseline. The frozen backbones collapse to majority-class predictions, while Neuro-JEPA on FLAIR shows modest but still limited discrimination. In contrast, the end-to-end trained ViT3D achieves higher accuracy and MCC on every task (up to 53.4% accuracy, MCC=0.27) and is the only model with non-trivial recall. Our findings suggest that these frozen neuroimaging foundation models are insufficient for fine-grained diagnostic classification in small, non-western clinical cohorts, motivating parameter-efficient adaptation and broader multi-site external validation for equitable deployment in global health settings.

</details>


### [127] [WebMRIQC: A Web-Based Implementation of MRIQC for Accessible MRI Image Quality Assessment in Resource-Constrained Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24014)
*Philip Nkwam,Ifeoluwa Oladeji,Sekinat Zurakat-Aderibigbe,Jasmine Cakmak,Harrison Aduluwa,Confidence Raymond,Cliff Mokua,Abdulrazaq Zubair,Daniel Champanda,Tolulope Olusuyi,Maruf Adewole,Udunna Anazodo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WebMRIQC：一个零安装、基于浏览器的MRI质量控制平台，封装容器化MRIQC并自动完成DICOM到BIDS转换、作业排队与交互式仪表盘，验证显示与原生MRIQC在多项IQM上高度一致，助力资源受限环境的标准化QC与跨中心协议优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工MRI质量评估主观且耗时；现有MRIQC虽标准化IQM提取，但依赖本地高性能计算与专业技能，限制在资源受限环境的推广。需要一种降低门槛、可在多中心和RCS中落地的QC方案。

Method: 构建WebMRIQC：在零安装网页端封装未改动的容器化MRIQC；自动执行去标识DICOM到BIDS转换；通过公平共享队列在共享计算节点上运行；产出交互式仪表盘，将IQM与文献阈值和开放数据集的基准分布对照；提出并实施一个验证框架，在BraTS-Africa与BraTS 2021数据集上比较WebMRIQC与原生MRIQC在13个IQM上的测量等效性。

Result: 初步结果显示在对比度、信号与噪声相关的多项IQM上，两者具有强一致性；平台成功实现端到端的网页化处理与基准化展示。

Conclusion: WebMRIQC降低了标准化MRI QC在RCS中的采用门槛，支持跨站点协议优化，并为区域化、协调一致的质量基准奠定基础；代码已开源以促进推广与复现。

Abstract: Reliable quality control (QC) of magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is essential for reliable diagnostic neuroimaging, yet standard manual assessment is subjective and time-consuming. MRIQC has established standardized automated extraction of image-quality metrics (IQMs), but its reliance on local computational imaging skills and capacity including high-performance computing, limits its adoption in resource-constrained settings (RCS). We present WebMRIQC (webmriqc.mailab.io), an open-source browser-based platform that wraps the validated MRIQC engine behind a zero-installation web interface. WebMRIQC automates the DICOM-to-BIDS conversion of de-identified MRI scans, executes the unmodified containerized MRIQC pipeline on a shared compute node governed by a fair-share job queue, and returns an interactive in-browser dashboard. The dashboard grounds every IQM in published quality thresholds, benchmarks each scan against the normative distribution of high-resource open datasets, and supports cross-site multicentre implementation of optimized scan protocols in RCS.We describe the system architecture and a validation framework establishing measurement equivalence between WebMRIQC and native MRIQC across thirteen IQMs on the BraTS-Africa and BraTS 2021 datasets. Preliminary results indicate strong agreement for contrast-, signal and noise-based metrics, demonstrating that web-based implementation lowers the barrier to standardized MRI QC and provides a foundation for harmonized, regionally adapted quality benchmarks across RCS imaging sites. The code is publicly available here https://github.com/CAMERA-MRI/WebMRIqc.

</details>


### [128] [InterHier: Learning Interconnected Hierarchical Semantics for Open-Vocabulary Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24026)
*Yeong-Jin Kim,Ho-Joong Kim,Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 InterHier，通过在层级提示前添加可学习上下文，并对其进行对齐优化，替代手工固定连接词，全面提升开放词汇目标检测中基于层级语义关系的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇检测常用手工固定连接词来连接上下位类别，以传递基类到新类的语义，但固定连接器难以准确表达复杂层级关系，限制对未见类别的泛化与匹配质量。

Method: 提出 InterHier：1) 构造“层级感知提示”，把上位/下位类别组合，并在最前加入可学习上下文；2) 通过优化该可学习上下文，使视觉区域嵌入与文本嵌入在共同空间对齐。该方案可无缝集成到现有开放词汇检测模型。

Result: 在多个开放词汇检测基准上，相比使用固定连接器的方法，InterHier持续提升性能，并达到与最新SOTA具有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: 用可学习上下文替代固定连接器能更好刻画层级语义，促进视觉-文本对齐，从而稳定提升开放词汇目标检测的泛化与精度，且方法通用、易集成。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the limitations of fixed, hand-crafted connectors in hierarchical semantic representations for open-vocabulary object detection. Existing methods establish semantic relationships between base categories and unseen novel categories by placing a fixed connector between adjacent super-/sub-categories. However, such fixed connectors may not optimally capture the relationships within a semantic hierarchy. To address this limitation, we propose interconnected hierarchical semantic representations (InterHier), which utilize a prepended learnable context to globally guide the interpretation of prompts containing hierarchical relationships. InterHier operates in two main stages. First, it constructs a hierarchy-aware prompt by integrating super-/sub-categories and prepending a learnable context. Second, it optimizes this learnable context to align visual region embeddings and textual embeddings. InterHier consistently improves performance over methods that rely on fixed connectors and can be seamlessly integrated into existing open-vocabulary object detection models. Experiments on open-vocabulary object detection benchmarks demonstrate that InterHier achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [129] [Video-STLayout Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24031)
*Akash Abdu Jyothi,Greg Mori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Video-STLayout预训练：用对比学习将视频编码器特征与由目标检测得到的时空布局（bounding boxes）编码器特征对齐，以学到更丰富的视频表征；在复杂场景的活动识别上有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频预训练常与语言或音频对齐，但在复杂场景中，动作识别需要细粒度的时空实体与关系信息。利用易得的目标检测框生成的时空布局，可提供与动作紧密相关的结构先验，提高表征的判别性与可迁移性。

Method: 1) 用现成检测器在视频帧上获得目标框与类别，构建时空布局序列；2) 训练一个布局编码器将这些布局编码成特征；3) 训练视频编码器，通过对比损失，使视频特征与对应布局特征对齐，与不匹配样本拉远；4) 形成跨模态（像素↔布局）的表征学习，不依赖文本/音频。

Result: 在复杂场景的活动识别任务上取得显著提升（论文摘要称有效），表明布局引导的预训练能提高下游性能与泛化。

Conclusion: 用检测框的时空布局作为监督信号进行跨模态对齐，是一种简单可行、无需额外标注的预训练途径，能学习到对复杂动作识别更有用的视频表征。

Abstract: In recent years, pre-training has become fundamental to learning effective video representations, enabling strong transfer to downstream tasks. A popular framework in pre-training involves aligning features of a video encoder with that of another modality, for example, language or audio. We introduce Video-STLayout pre-training, a novel strategy for obtaining rich video representations informed by spatio-temporal layout of object bounding boxes. Object layouts can easily be obtained by applying an off-the-shelf object detector on the video frames. Our method uses a contrastive loss to align video features with the layout features from a trained layout encoder. We show the effectiveness of our approach in the task of activity recognition in complex scenes.

</details>


### [130] [U-PEN Mamba: Progressive Expansion with Selective State-Space Modeling for Efficient Retinal Vessel Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24049)
*Abel A. Reyes-Angulo,Sidike Paheding,Vijayan K. Asari,Mohammad Alam,Jeevan Devagiri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出U-PEN Mamba，一种结合渐进式非线性特征扩展与选择性状态空间（Mamba）全局建模的U形视网膜血管分割网络，在CHASE_DB1与DRIVE上以较少参数取得最佳mIoU与优良Dice。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视网膜血管分割受细小血管、低对比度与前景-背景极度不平衡影响，传统编码-解码网络难以兼顾局部细节与全局依赖，且Transformer类全局建模常带来高计算与参数开销，需一种参数高效且能捕获长程依赖的方法。

Method: 在U形架构中：1) 渐进式非线性特征扩展模块，逐层丰富局部血管响应；2) Mamba Global Context（MGC）块，以选择性状态空间模型线性序列复杂度建模长程空间依赖；3) 基于注意力的解码器特征融合，强化精细血管边界恢复；配合一致的patch级预处理与多类基线比较与消融。

Result: 在CHASE_DB1与DRIVE上分别获得mIoU 0.8394/0.8221，Dice 0.8187/0.8078，参数量约21.6M；消融显示MGC对U-Net基线贡献最大增益，投影维度与state size可在精度与效率间调节。

Conclusion: 选择性状态空间建模作为全局上下文机制在视网膜血管分割中参数高效且效果显著；U-PEN Mamba在多基线上取得最优或接近最优表现，并提供实用的精度-效率权衡；代码已开源以便复现与扩展。

Abstract: Accurate retinal vessel segmentation is important for computer-aided ophthalmic analysis, yet thin vessels, low contrast, and severe foreground-background imbalance remain challenging for encoder-decoder networks. This paper presents U-PEN Mamba, a U-shaped retinal vessel segmentation architecture that couples progressive nonlinear feature expansion with selective state-space modeling. The proposed network enriches local vessel responses with progressive expansion, models long-range spatial dependencies through a Mamba Global Context (MGC) block with linear sequence complexity, and uses attention-based decoder fusion to recover fine vascular boundaries. We evaluate U-PEN Mamba on CHASE DB1 and DRIVE using a consistent patch-based preprocessing pipeline and compare it with convolutional, attention-based, transformer-based, and Mamba-based segmentation baselines. U-PEN Mamba obtains the best mean intersection over union among the compared methods, achieving 0.8394 on CHASE DB1 and 0.8221 on DRIVE, with Dice scores of 0.8187 and 0.8078, respectively, using 21.6M trainable parameters. Ablation studies show that the MGC block contributes the largest gain over the U-Net baseline, while projection dimension and state size provide practical accuracy-efficiency control. These results indicate that selective state-space modeling is a promising global-context mechanism for parameter-efficient retinal vessel segmentation. Code is available at: https://github.com/areyesan/UPEN_Mamba.

</details>


### [131] [All-in-One Multilingual Scene Text Recognition with Script-aware Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24058)
*Xingsong Ye,Yongkun Du,Jiaxin Zhang,Zhixian Li,Chong Sun,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Lianwen Jin,Zhineng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量高准确的多语言场景文字识别方案：构建10脚本/229语言的合成数据集TextMuSS-10M，并设计脚本感知的Mixture-of-Experts（ScriptMoE）解码器，实现单模型多脚本识别，达到SOTA准确率并以更少参数超越大型VLM与现有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言场景文字识别困难在于多数语言缺少训练数据，且用单一模型兼容多脚本性能受限；现有方案要么为每种语言训练独立识别器，成本高且误差累积，要么依赖大规模VLM，成本高且对多脚本仍不够准确。

Method: 1) 数据：构建大规模合成数据集TextMuSS-10M，覆盖10种文字脚本、229种语言，提供均衡且充足的监督；2) 模型：提出ScriptMoE，采用共享视觉编码器与稀疏MoE解码块。图像级路由器将输入分派给与脚本对齐的top-2专家，同时设置共享专家吸收跨脚本知识，实现脚本感知的专家选择与知识共享；3) 评测：在自建TextMuSS-Bench与CC-OCR端到端任务上进行对比实验。

Result: 在TextMuSS-Bench（10脚本，10,899图像）上达到82.06%准确率，比最强STR基线高1.31%；在CC-OCR多语言端到端任务中，仅替换PP-OCRv5的识别器为ScriptMoE，F1由65.71%提升到80.89%，以更少参数小幅超越最佳VLM（80.73%）。

Conclusion: 合成的多脚本大数据与脚本感知的稀疏MoE解码器可在单模型内有效处理多语言场景文本识别，兼顾精度与效率，优于按语言专门化模型和大规模VLM，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Multilingual scene text recognition (STR) remains challenging due to the scarcity of training data for most languages and the difficulty of serving diverse scripts within a single model. Existing solutions either deploy one recognizer per language, inflating cost and introducing error accumulation, or rely on massive vision-language models (VLMs) that are expensive and still inaccurate on many scripts. In this work, we pursue an all-in-one multilingual recognizer that is simpler than per-language experts, lighter than VLMs, and more accurate than both. First, we construct TextMuSS-10M, a large-scale synthetic scene text dataset spanning 10 scripts and 229 languages. It provides balanced and sufficient supervision where real data is unavailable. Second, we propose ScriptMoE, a script-aware Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture. It shares a single visual encoder and replaces the dense decoder with a sparse MoE block, which consists of an image-level router dispatches each image to the top-2 script-aligned experts and a shared expert absorbs cross-script knowledge. Extensive experiments on our assembled TextMuSS-Bench (10 scripts, 10,899 images) show that ScriptMoE achieves the highest accuracy of 82.06%, outperforming the strongest STR baseline by 1.31%. On the CC-OCR end-to-end multilingual task, replacing only the recognizer in PP-OCRv5 with ScriptMoE lifts F1 score from 65.71% to 80.89%, slightly surpassing the best VLM (80.73%) at a fraction of the parameter count.

</details>


### [132] [Vision Transformers versus convolutional neural networks for fine-grained orchid genus identification in a species-rich, data-poor flora: a controlled benchmark on the Orchidaceae of New Guinea](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24064)
*Reza Saputra,Diah Harnoni Apriyanti,André Schuiteman,Kurt Metzger,Ashley Field,Katharina Nargar,William Edwards*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出并评估一个用于新几内亚极度物种丰富且数据稀缺的兰科图像识别系统：先按属分类、再基于嵌入检索相似物种；自监督ViT（DINOv2）最佳，并在检索与开放集检测上表现强。系统已上线应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新几内亚岛拥有极其丰富的兰花物种，但每个物种的图像样本极少，难以进行细粒度的物种级识别。现有方法与预训练策略在这种“物种超多、数据超稀”的场景下哪种最有效尚不明确，需要一个可部署、可泛化的解决方案。

Method: 构建两阶段系统：1) 用预训练主干微调后进行属级分类；2) 用FAISS基于图像嵌入检索候选物种的相似参考图像。对四个预训练主干进行统一协议下的比较：两种ViT（DINOv2、BioCLIP 2）与两种CNN（ConvNeXt V2-L、EfficientNetV2-L）。在固定的物种分层数据划分（16,701张图，120属、1,350种）上评估：准确率、校准、错误结构、物种检索性能，以及对未见属的开放集检测。采用温度标定与基于距离的开放集门控。

Result: DINOv2属级表现最佳（macro top-1 66.9%，全局top-1 88.9%）；两种ViT均优于两种CNN；通用自监督预训练（DINOv2）较生物领域预训练（BioCLIP 2）macro top-1高7.1点。错误集中在两个样本丰富的“错误吸引子”属。DINOv2嵌入的Recall@5：物种86.6%，属98.7%。温度标定将各主干的ECE降至约0.03。距离式开放集门控对未见属的AUROC为0.958。

Conclusion: 自监督ViT结合嵌入检索在超多物种、少样本植物群落中有效且可部署。该方法为其他高度多样而记录不足的类群提供了可复用模板；系统已作为“新几内亚兰花识别器”上线。

Abstract: New Guinea is the world's richest island flora (~2,856 orchid species), yet most species are represented by only a handful of photographs, far fewer than direct species-level classification requires. Methods for fine-grained identification in such species-rich, data-poor floras are needed, and it remains unclear which backbone architecture and pretraining strategy best support them. We built a two-stage system that first predicts the genus of a query photograph, then retrieves visually similar reference images of candidate species using FAISS. We compared four pretrained backbones -- two Vision Transformers (ViTs; DINOv2, BioCLIP 2) and two CNNs (ConvNeXt V2-L, EfficientNetV2-L) -- fine-tuned under an identical protocol on a fixed, species-stratified partition of 16,701 photographs spanning 120 genera and 1,350 species, assessing accuracy, calibration, error structure, species retrieval, and open-set detection of novel genera. DINOv2 attained the best genus performance (macro top-1 66.9%, 95% CI 63.7-70.6; global top-1 88.9%); both ViTs outranked both CNNs, and general-purpose self-supervised pretraining (DINOv2) outperformed domain-matched biological pretraining (BioCLIP 2) by 7.1 points of macro top-1. Errors concentrated on two abundant genera acting as error attractors. DINOv2 embeddings achieved species Recall@5 of 86.6% and genus Recall@5 of 98.7%; temperature scaling reduced every backbone's Expected Calibration Error to about 0.03; and a distance-based open-set gate flagged unseen genera (mean AUROC 0.958). A self-supervised Vision-Transformer backbone combined with embedding retrieval is an effective, deployable strategy for fine-grained identification in species-rich, data-poor floras. The system is released as an open web application (the New Guinea Orchid Identifier), offering a practical template for other hyperdiverse, under-documented taxa.

</details>


### [133] [Monitorable Chart Reasoning Agents via Verifiable Process Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24071)
*Sanchit Sinha,Oana Frunza,Kashif Rasul,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Chart-RVR：一种用可验证过程奖励训练的可监控图表推理智能体，分解为结构识别、证据重建与推导三步，在多基准上提升准确率与可验证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大视觉语言模型的图表代理虽在基准上准确，但缺乏可审计性：要么仅给答案，要么给难以验证的自由文本理由，难以定位错误来源（读图、取值或计算）。部署需要可被审核与验证的推理过程。

Method: 提出强化学习框架Chart-RVR，引入可验证的过程奖励。将图表推理拆为三块：Structure（识别图表类型）、Evidence（用JSON重建底层数据表）、Derivation（逐步计算得到答案的轨迹）。训练与评估结合多基准，使用三角测量的可监控性评估协议（真值代理指标、oracle信息增益、LLM审计的过程可验证性与证据定位）。

Result: 在六个域内与域外基准上，相比同规模LVLM达到SOTA准确率；其生成的理由更可验证且证据更扎实，优于CoT提示、监督微调和现有图表专用基线。

Conclusion: 通过将图表推理模块化并用可验证过程奖励优化，Chart-RVR同时提升了准确率与可监控性，支持在关键场景中更可靠的审计与部署。

Abstract: Chart reasoning agents are increasingly used to extract actionable insights in critical domains, achieving state-of-the-art performance on multiple benchmarks. Yet, high benchmark accuracy alone is insufficient for deployment, where stakeholders must be able to audit and verify how a model reaches its answer. Existing LVLM-based chart agents produce either answer-only predictions or free-form rationales that are hard to verify, obscuring whether an error arose from misreading the chart, extracting a wrong value, or miscomputing. We propose Chart-RVR, a reinforcement learning framework for training monitorable chart agents with verifiable process rewards. Chart-RVR decomposes chart reasoning into three auditable blocks: Structure, identifying the chart type; Evidence, reconstructing the underlying data table in JSON; and Derivation, exposing the stepwise trace that computes the answer. Across six in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks, Chart-RVR attains state-of-the-art accuracy among comparable-sized LVLMs. Beyond accuracy, we assess monitorability using a triangulated protocol that combines ground-truth surrogate metrics, an oracle information-gain measure, and an LLM-as-auditor scoring Process Verifiability and Evidence Localization, showing that Chart-RVR yields rationales that are markedly more verifiable and evidence-grounded than those from CoT prompting, SFT, and existing chart-specific baselines.

</details>


### [134] [A paired synthetic construction-site image dataset for robust computer vision under adverse conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24075)
*Viet Huy Duong,Ruoxin Xiong,Md Abdullah Al Forhad,Weishi Shi*

Main category: cs.CV
